#### Lists of Three Types ^1-0a-0

1. (A) things good for the mind.
(B) things bad for the mind.
(C) things neither good nor bad for the mind. ^1-0a-1

2. (A) things connected with a pleasant feeling.
(B) things connected with a painful feeling.
(C) things connected with a feeling that is neither painful nor pleasant. ^1-0a-2

3. (A) things that are results of action.
(B) things that are result-states.
(C) things that are neither results nor result-states. ^1-0a-3

4. (A) things grasped and that can be grasped.
(B) things not grasped that can be grasped.
(C) things not grasped and that cannot be grasped. ^1-0a-4

5. (A) things that are stained and that stain.
(B) things not stained that stain.
(C) things not stained and that do not stain. ^1-0a-5

6. (A) things with putting the mind onto something and keeping the mind on something.
(B) things with only keeping the mind on something.
(C) things without putting the mind onto something or keeping the mind on something. ^1-0a-6

7. (A) things accompanied by a feeling of joy.
(B) things accompanied by comfort.
(C) things accompanied by calm balance. ^1-0a-7

8. (A) things that can be removed by insight.
(B) things that can be removed by developing practice.
(C) things that cannot be removed by insight or by developing practice. ^1-0a-8

9. (A) things that are causes for removal by insight.
(B) things that are causes for removal by developing practice.
(C) things that are causes for removal neither by insight nor by developing practice. ^1-0a-9

10. (A) things that lead to growth.
(B) things that lead to decline.
(C) things that lead neither to growth nor to decline. ^1-0a-10

11. (A) things for those still in training.
(B) things for those who have finished training.
(C) things neither for those in training nor for those who have finished training. ^1-0a-11

12. (A) limited things.
(B) elevated things.
(C) immeasurable things. ^1-0a-12

13. (A) things with a limited focus.
(B) things with an elevated focus.
(C) things with an immeasurable focus. ^1-0a-13

14. (A) inferior things.
(B) middle things.
(C) superior things. ^1-0a-14

15. (A) things fixed on what is wrong.
(B) things fixed on what is right.
(C) things not fixed either way. ^1-0a-15

16. (A) things focused on the path.
(B) things that are causes of the path.
(C) things that lead the path. ^1-0a-16

17. (A) things that have arisen.
(B) things that have not arisen.
(C) things that produce arising. ^1-0a-17

18. (A) past things.
(B) future things.
(C) present things. ^1-0a-18

19. (A) things focused on the past.
(B) things focused on the future.
(C) things focused on the present. ^1-0a-19

20. (A) inner things.
(B) outer things.
(C) inner and outer things. ^1-0a-20

21. (A) things focused on what is inner.
(B) things focused on what is outer.
(C) things focused on what is inner and outer. ^1-0a-21

22. (A) things that can be seen and that push back when touched.
(B) things that cannot be seen but push back when touched.
(C) things that cannot be seen and do not push back when touched.
Lists of Three Types. ^1-0a-22

### Section on Mind States ^1-1-0

#### Wholesome Mind in the Sense-Desire Level ^1-1-1-0

##### Word Analysis ^1-1-1-1-0

1. What things are good for the mind? At a time when a wholesome mind of the sense-desire level has arisen, accompanied by a happy mental feeling and associated with knowledge, focused on a visible form, or a sound, or a smell, or a taste, or a touch, or a mental object, or on whatever it may be based — at that time there is contact, feeling, perception, intention, mind, putting the mind onto something, keeping the mind on something, a feeling of joy, comfort, one-pointedness of mind, power of faith, power of effort, power of paying close attention, power of calm focus, power of wisdom, power of the mind, power of happy feeling, power of life, right view, right intention, right effort, right paying close attention, right calm focus, strength of faith, strength of effort, strength of paying close attention, strength of calm focus, strength of wisdom, strength of conscience, strength of worry about wrong action, non-greed, non-hatred, wisdom without delusion, not coveting, not wishing harm, right view, conscience, worry about wrong action, calm in mental-body factors, calm in the mind, lightness in mental-body factors, lightness in the mind, softness in mental-body factors, softness in the mind, readiness in mental-body factors, readiness in the mind, fitness in mental-body factors, fitness in the mind, straightness in mental-body factors, straightness in the mind, paying close attention, knowing what we are doing, calm stillness, clear seeing insight, striving, calmness, and whatever other formless things that arise from causes are present at that time — these things are good for the mind. ^1-1

2. What is contact at that time? The contact at that time that is touching, contacting, and the state of being contacted — that is contact at that time. ^1-2

3. What is feeling at that time? The pleasant mental comfort and happy feeling born of mind-contact at that time — that is feeling at that time. ^1-3

4. What is perception at that time? The perceiving and state of having perceived born of mind-contact at that time — that is perception at that time. ^1-4

5. What is intention at that time? The intending and state of having intended born of mind-contact at that time — that is intention at that time. ^1-5

6. What is mind at that time? The mind, heart, and consciousness that is the mind element at that time — that is mind at that time. ^1-6

7. What is putting the mind onto something at that time? The placing and directing of the mind in a right way at that time — that is putting the mind onto something at that time. ^1-7

8. What is keeping the mind on something at that time? The following along, exploring, and looking over of the mind at that time — that is keeping the mind on something at that time. ^1-8

9. What is a feeling of joy at that time? The joy, gladness, delight, and lightness in the mind at that time — that is a feeling of joy at that time. ^1-9

10. What is comfort at that time? The pleasant mental comfort and happy feeling born of mind-contact at that time — that is comfort at that time. ^1-10

11. What is one-pointedness of mind at that time? The steadiness, settling, and calm focus of the mind without distraction at that time — that is one-pointedness of mind at that time. ^1-11

12. What is the power of faith at that time? The faith, trust, and confidence at that time — that is the power of faith at that time. ^1-12

13. What is the power of effort at that time? The starting, striving, and not giving up in the mind at that time — that is the power of effort at that time. ^1-13

14. What is the power of paying close attention at that time? The remembering and not forgetting at that time — that is the power of paying close attention at that time. ^1-14

15. What is the power of calm focus at that time? The steadiness and settling of the mind without distraction at that time — that is the power of calm focus at that time. ^1-15

16. What is the power of wisdom at that time? The wisdom that examines closely and understands at that time — that is the power of wisdom at that time. ^1-16

17. What is the power of the mind at that time? The mind that knows at that time — that is the power of the mind at that time. ^1-17

18. What is the power of happy feeling at that time? The pleasant mental comfort in the mind at that time — that is the power of happy feeling at that time. ^1-18

19. What is the power of life at that time? The life that sustains those formless things at that time — that is the power of life at that time. ^1-19

20. What is right view at that time? The wisdom that understands in the right way at that time — that is right view at that time. ^1-20

21. What is right intention at that time? Putting the mind onto something in the right way at that time — that is right intention at that time. ^1-21

22. What is right effort at that time? The effort that strives in the right way at that time — that is right effort at that time. ^1-22

23. What is right paying close attention at that time? Paying close attention in the right way at that time — that is right paying close attention at that time. ^1-23

24. What is right calm focus at that time? The mind staying on one thing in the right way at that time — that is right calm focus at that time. ^1-24

25. What is the strength of faith at that time? The faith at that time — that is the strength of faith at that time. ^1-25

26. What is the strength of effort at that time? The effort at that time — that is the strength of effort at that time. ^1-26

27. What is the strength of paying close attention at that time? The paying close attention at that time — that is the strength of paying close attention at that time. ^1-27

28. What is the strength of calm focus at that time? The mind staying on one thing at that time — that is the strength of calm focus at that time. ^1-28

29. What is the strength of wisdom at that time? The wisdom at that time — that is the strength of wisdom at that time. ^1-29

30. What is the strength of conscience at that time? Feeling conscience when one should, about things bad for the mind — that is the strength of conscience at that time. ^1-30

31. What is the strength of worry about wrong action at that time? Worrying when one should, about things bad for the mind — that is the strength of worry about wrong action at that time. ^1-31

32. What is non-greed at that time? Not being greedy — a root of what is good for the mind — that is non-greed at that time. ^1-32

33. What is non-hatred at that time? Not hating or wishing harm — a root of what is good for the mind — that is non-hatred at that time? ^1-33

34. What is wisdom without delusion at that time? The wisdom that is not dull — a root of what is good for the mind — that is wisdom without delusion at that time. ^1-34

35. What is not coveting at that time? Non-greed at that time — that is not coveting at that time. ^1-35

36. What is not wishing harm at that time? Non-hatred at that time — that is not wishing harm at that time. ^1-36

37. What is right view at that time? The wisdom that understands — that is right view at that time. ^1-37

38. What is conscience at that time? Feeling conscience when one should, about things bad for the mind — that is conscience at that time. ^1-38

39. What is worry about wrong action at that time? Worrying when one should, about things bad for the mind — that is worry about wrong action at that time. ^1-39

Eighth Mind.
Eight Great Wholesome Minds of the Sense-Desire Level.
Second Recitation Round. ^1-159

#### Wholesome Mind in the Form Level ^1-1-2-0

##### Fourfold Method ^1-1-2-1-0

160. What things are good for the mind? At a time when one develops the path for reaching the form level, fully separated from sense desire, separated from things bad for the mind, with putting the mind onto something and keeping the mind on something, born of seclusion, with a feeling of joy and comfort — reaching and dwelling in the first state of deep calm in meditation on the earth meditation object — at that time there is contact … calmness, and whatever other formless things that arise from causes are present at that time — these things are good for the mind. ^1-160

161. What things are good for the mind? At a time when one develops the path for reaching the form level, with the calming of putting the mind onto something and keeping the mind on something, inner peace, one-pointedness of mind, without putting the mind onto something, without keeping the mind on something, born of deep calm, with a feeling of joy and comfort — reaching and dwelling in the second state of deep calm in meditation on the earth meditation object — at that time there is contact, feeling, perception, intention, mind, a feeling of joy, comfort, one-pointedness of mind, power of faith, power of effort, power of paying close attention, power of calm focus, power of wisdom, power of the mind, power of happy feeling, power of life, right view, right effort … striving, calmness, and whatever other formless things that arise from causes are present at that time — these things are good for the mind …
At that time there are four aggregates, two sense bases, two elements, three foods, eight powers, three-part deep calm, four-part path, seven strengths, three causes, one contact … one mental-object element; and whatever other formless things that arise from causes are present at that time — these things are good for the mind … ^1-161

277. What things are good for the mind? At a time when one develops supramundane deep calm, leading out, tending to the decline of defilements, for the abandoning of wrong views, for attainment of the first stage, fully separated from sense desire … reaching and dwelling in the first state of deep calm in meditation by the painful way of practice with slow understanding — at that time there is contact, feeling, perception, intention, mind, putting the mind onto something, keeping the mind on something, a feeling of joy, comfort, one-pointedness of mind, power of faith, power of effort, power of paying close attention, power of calm focus, power of wisdom, power of the mind, power of happy feeling, power of life, power of one who will fully know, right view, right intention, right speech, right action, right livelihood, right effort, right paying close attention, right calm focus, strength of faith, strength of effort, strength of paying close attention, strength of calm focus, strength of wisdom, strength of conscience, strength of worry about wrong action, non-greed, non-hatred, wisdom without delusion, not coveting, not wishing harm, right view, conscience, worry about wrong action, calm in mental-body factors, calm in the mind, lightness in mental-body factors, lightness in the mind, softness in mental-body factors, softness in the mind, readiness in mental-body factors, readiness in the mind, fitness in mental-body factors, fitness in the mind, straightness in mental-body factors, straightness in the mind, paying close attention, knowing what we are doing, calm stillness, clear seeing insight, striving, calmness, and whatever other formless things that arise from causes are present at that time — these things are good for the mind. ^1-277

278. What is contact at that time? The contact at that time that is touching, contacting, and the state of being contacted — that is contact at that time. ^1-278

279. What is feeling at that time? The pleasant mental comfort and happy feeling born of mind-contact at that time — that is feeling at that time. ^1-279

280. What is perception at that time? The perceiving and state of having perceived born of mind-contact at that time — that is perception at that time. ^1-280

281. What is intention at that time? The intending and state of having intended born of mind-contact at that time — that is intention at that time. ^1-281

282. What is mind at that time? The mind, heart, and consciousness that is the mind element at that time — that is mind at that time. ^1-282

283. What is putting the mind onto something at that time? Putting the mind onto something in a right way that is a path factor included in the path — that is putting the mind onto something at that time. ^1-283

284. What is keeping the mind on something at that time? The following along, exploring, and looking over of the mind at that time — that is keeping the mind on something at that time. ^1-284

285. What is a feeling of joy at that time? The joy, gladness, delight, and lightness in the mind that is the factor of awakening for joy — that is a feeling of joy at that time. ^1-285

286. What is comfort at that time? The pleasant mental comfort and happy feeling born of mind-contact at that time — that is comfort at that time. ^1-286

287. What is one-pointedness of mind at that time? The steadiness, settling, and calm focus of the mind without distraction that is the factor of awakening for calm focus, a path factor included in the path — that is one-pointedness of mind at that time. ^1-287

288. What is the power of faith at that time? The faith, trust, and confidence at that time — that is the power of faith at that time. ^1-288

289. What is the power of effort at that time? The starting, striving, and not giving up in the mind that is right effort, the factor of awakening for effort, a path factor included in the path — that is the power of effort at that time. ^1-289

290. What is the power of paying close attention at that time? The remembering and not forgetting that is right paying close attention, the factor of awakening for paying close attention, a path factor included in the path — that is the power of paying close attention at that time. ^1-290

291. What is the power of calm focus at that time? The steadiness and settling of the mind without distraction that is right calm focus, the factor of awakening for calm focus, a path factor included in the path — that is the power of calm focus at that time. ^1-291

292. What is the power of wisdom at that time? The wisdom that examines closely and understands that is right view, the factor of awakening for investigation of things, a path factor included in the path — that is the power of wisdom at that time. ^1-292

293. What is the power of the mind at that time? The mind that knows at that time — that is the power of the mind at that time. ^1-293

294. What is the power of happy feeling at that time? The pleasant mental comfort in the mind at that time — that is the power of happy feeling at that time. ^1-294

295. What is the power of life at that time? The life that sustains those formless things at that time — that is the power of life at that time. ^1-295

296. What is the power of one who will fully know at that time? The wisdom for realizing what has not yet been seen, attained, known, or experienced — that is right view, the factor of awakening for investigation of things, a path factor included in the path — that is the power of one who will fully know at that time. ^1-296

297. What is right view at that time? The wisdom that understands in the right way, the factor of awakening for investigation of things, a path factor included in the path — that is right view at that time. ^1-297

298. What is right intention at that time? Putting the mind onto something in a right way that is a path factor included in the path — that is right intention at that time. ^1-298

299. What is right speech at that time? Refraining from the four wrong ways of speaking — turning away, not doing, not committing wrong, not overstepping the limit, blocking the wrong path — right speech that is a path factor included in the path — that is right speech at that time. ^1-299

300. What is right action at that time? Refraining from the three wrong bodily actions — turning away, not doing, not committing wrong, not overstepping the limit, blocking the wrong path — right action that is a path factor included in the path — that is right action at that time. ^1-300

301. What is right livelihood at that time? Refraining from wrong livelihood — turning away, not doing, not committing wrong, not overstepping the limit, blocking the wrong path — right livelihood that is a path factor included in the path — that is right livelihood at that time. ^1-301

302. What is right effort at that time? The effort that strives in the right way, the factor of awakening for effort, a path factor included in the path — that is right effort at that time. ^1-302

303. What is right paying close attention at that time? Paying close attention in the right way, the factor of awakening for paying close attention, a path factor included in the path — that is right paying close attention at that time. ^1-303

304. What is right calm focus at that time? The mind staying on one thing in the right way, the factor of awakening for calm focus, a path factor included in the path — that is right calm focus at that time. ^1-304

305. What is the strength of faith at that time? The faith at that time — that is the strength of faith at that time. ^1-305

306. What is the strength of effort at that time? The effort that is right effort, the factor of awakening for effort, a path factor included in the path — that is the strength of effort at that time. ^1-306

307. What is the strength of paying close attention at that time? The paying close attention that is right paying close attention, the factor of awakening for paying close attention, a path factor included in the path — that is the strength of paying close attention at that time. ^1-307

308. What is the strength of calm focus at that time? The calm focus that is right calm focus, the factor of awakening for calm focus, a path factor included in the path — that is the strength of calm focus at that time. ^1-308

309. What is the strength of wisdom at that time? The wisdom that is right view, the factor of awakening for investigation of things, a path factor included in the path — that is the strength of wisdom at that time. ^1-309

310. What is the strength of conscience at that time? Feeling conscience when one should, about things bad for the mind — that is the strength of conscience at that time. ^1-310

311. What is the strength of worry about wrong action at that time? Worrying when one should, about things bad for the mind — that is the strength of worry about wrong action at that time. ^1-311

312. What is non-greed at that time? Not being greedy — a root of what is good for the mind — that is non-greed at that time. ^1-312

313. What is non-hatred at that time? Not hating or wishing harm — a root of what is good for the mind — that is non-hatred at that time. ^1-313

314. What is wisdom without delusion at that time? The wisdom that is not dull, right view, the factor of awakening for investigation of things, a path factor included in the path — that is wisdom without delusion at that time. ^1-314

315. What is not coveting at that time? Non-greed at that time — that is not coveting at that time. ^1-315

316. What is not wishing harm at that time? Non-hatred at that time — that is not wishing harm at that time. ^1-316

317. What is right view at that time? The wisdom that understands, the factor of awakening for investigation of things, a path factor included in the path — that is right view at that time. ^1-317

318. What is conscience at that time? Feeling conscience when one should, about things bad for the mind — that is conscience at that time. ^1-318

319. What is worry about wrong action at that time? Worrying when one should, about things bad for the mind — that is worry about wrong action at that time. ^1-319

320. What is calm in mental-body factors at that time? The calm in the aggregates of feeling, perception, and habits and intentions that is the factor of awakening for calm — that is calm in mental-body factors at that time. ^1-320

321. What is calm in the mind at that time? The calm in the aggregate of consciousness that is the factor of awakening for calm — that is calm in the mind at that time. ^1-321

322. What is lightness in mental-body factors at that time? The lightness in the aggregates of feeling, perception, and habits and intentions — that is lightness in mental-body factors at that time. ^1-322

323. What is lightness in the mind at that time? The lightness in the aggregate of consciousness — that is lightness in the mind at that time. ^1-323

324. What is softness in mental-body factors at that time? The softness in the aggregates of feeling, perception, and habits and intentions — that is softness in mental-body factors at that time. ^1-324

325. What is softness in the mind at that time? The softness in the aggregate of consciousness — that is softness in the mind at that time. ^1-325

326. What is readiness in mental-body factors at that time? The readiness in the aggregates of feeling, perception, and habits and intentions — that is readiness in mental-body factors at that time. ^1-326

327. What is readiness in the mind at that time? The readiness in the aggregate of consciousness — that is readiness in the mind at that time. ^1-327

328. What is fitness in mental-body factors at that time? The fitness in the aggregates of feeling, perception, and habits and intentions — that is fitness in mental-body factors at that time. ^1-328

329. What is fitness in the mind at that time? The fitness in the aggregate of consciousness — that is fitness in the mind at that time. ^1-329

330. What is straightness in mental-body factors at that time? The straightness in the aggregates of feeling, perception, and habits and intentions — that is straightness in mental-body factors at that time. ^1-330

331. What is straightness in the mind at that time? The straightness in the aggregate of consciousness — that is straightness in the mind at that time. ^1-331

332. What is paying close attention at that time? The paying close attention that is right paying close attention, the factor of awakening for paying close attention, a path factor included in the path — that is paying close attention at that time. ^1-332

333. What is knowing what we are doing at that time? The wisdom that knows clearly, right view, the factor of awakening for investigation of things, a path factor included in the path — that is knowing what we are doing at that time. ^1-333

334. What is calm stillness at that time? The mind staying on one thing that is right calm focus, the factor of awakening for calm focus, a path factor included in the path — that is calm stillness at that time. ^1-334

335. What is clear seeing insight at that time? The wisdom that sees clearly, right view, the factor of awakening for investigation of things, a path factor included in the path — that is clear seeing insight at that time. ^1-335

336. What is striving at that time? The effort that is right effort, the factor of awakening for effort, a path factor included in the path — that is striving at that time. ^1-336

337. What is calmness at that time? The mind staying on one thing without wandering that is right calm focus, the factor of awakening for calm focus, a path factor included in the path — that is calmness at that time. Whatever other formless things that arise from causes are present at that time are also good for the mind.
At that time there are four aggregates, two sense bases, two elements, three foods, nine powers, five-part deep calm, eight-part path, seven strengths, three causes, one contact … one mental-object element; and whatever other formless things that arise from causes are present at that time — these things are good for the mind … ^1-337

338. What is the aggregate of habits and intentions at that time? Contact, intention, putting the mind onto something, keeping the mind on something, a feeling of joy, one-pointedness of mind, power of faith, power of effort, power of paying close attention, power of calm focus, power of wisdom, power of life, power of one who will fully know, right view, right intention, right speech, right action, right livelihood, right effort, right paying close attention, right calm focus, strength of faith, strength of effort, strength of paying close attention, strength of calm focus, strength of wisdom, strength of conscience, strength of worry about wrong action, non-greed, non-hatred, wisdom without delusion, not coveting, not wishing harm, right view, conscience, worry about wrong action, calm in mental-body factors, calm in the mind, lightness in mental-body factors, lightness in the mind, softness in mental-body factors, softness in the mind, readiness in mental-body factors, readiness in the mind, fitness in mental-body factors, fitness in the mind, straightness in mental-body factors, straightness in the mind, paying close attention, knowing what we are doing, calm stillness, clear seeing insight, striving, calmness, and whatever other formless things that arise from causes are present at that time, except the aggregate of feeling, except the aggregate of perception, except the aggregate of consciousness — that is the aggregate of habits and intentions at that time … these things are good for the mind. ^1-338

339. What things are good for the mind? At a time when one develops supramundane deep calm, leading out, tending to the decline of defilements, for the abandoning of wrong views, for attainment of the first stage, fully separated from sense desire … reaching and dwelling in the first state of deep calm in meditation by the painful way of practice with quick understanding — at that time there is contact … calmness, and whatever other formless things that arise from causes are present at that time — these things are good for the mind. ^1-339

Supramundane Mind. ^1-364

#### Twelve Unwholesome Minds ^1-1-6-0

365. What things are bad for the mind? At a time when an unwholesome mind has arisen, accompanied by a happy mental feeling and associated with wrong view, focused on a visible form, or a sound, or a smell, or a taste, or a touch, or a mental object, or on whatever it may be based — at that time there is contact, feeling, perception, intention, mind, putting the mind onto something, keeping the mind on something, a feeling of joy, comfort, one-pointedness of mind, power of effort, power of calm focus, power of the mind, power of happy feeling, power of life, wrong view, wrong intention, wrong effort, wrong calm focus, strength of effort, strength of calm focus, strength of not feeling conscience, strength of not worrying about wrong action, greed, delusion, coveting, wrong view, not feeling conscience, not worrying about wrong action, calmness, striving, and freedom from distraction, and whatever other formless things that arise from causes are present at that time — these things are bad for the mind. ^1-365

366. What is contact at that time? The contact at that time that is touching, contacting, and the state of being contacted — that is contact at that time. ^1-366

367. What is feeling at that time? The pleasant mental comfort and happy feeling born of mind-contact at that time — that is feeling at that time. ^1-367

368. What is perception at that time? The perceiving and state of having perceived born of mind-contact at that time — that is perception at that time. ^1-368

369. What is intention at that time? The intending and state of having intended born of mind-contact at that time — that is intention at that time. ^1-369

370. What is mind at that time? The mind, heart, and consciousness that is the mind-consciousness element at that time — that is mind at that time. ^1-370

371. What is putting the mind onto something at that time? The placing and directing of the mind in a wrong way at that time — that is putting the mind onto something at that time. ^1-371

372. What is keeping the mind on something at that time? The following along, exploring, and looking over of the mind at that time — that is keeping the mind on something at that time. ^1-372

373. What is a feeling of joy at that time? The joy, gladness, delight, and lightness in the mind at that time — that is a feeling of joy at that time. ^1-373

374. What is comfort at that time? The pleasant mental comfort and happy feeling born of mind-contact at that time — that is comfort at that time. ^1-374

375. What is one-pointedness of mind at that time? The steadiness, settling, and wrong calm focus of the mind without distraction at that time — that is one-pointedness of mind at that time. ^1-375

376. What is the power of effort at that time? The starting, striving, and not giving up in the mind that is wrong effort at that time — that is the power of effort at that time. ^1-376

377. What is the power of calm focus at that time? The steadiness and settling of the mind without distraction that is wrong calm focus at that time — that is the power of calm focus at that time? ^1-377

378. What is the power of the mind at that time? The mind that knows at that time — that is the power of the mind at that time. ^1-378

379. What is the power of happy feeling at that time? The pleasant mental comfort in the mind at that time — that is the power of happy feeling at that time. ^1-379

380. What is the power of life at that time? The life that sustains those formless things at that time — that is the power of life at that time. ^1-380

381. What is wrong view at that time? The wrong view, wrong fixed idea, fixed idea that traps, fence of wrong view, twisting toward wrong view, trembling of wrong view, fetter of wrong view, grasping, standing firmly, entering deeply, taking as true, wrong path, wrongness, belonging to a wrong sect, and grasping what is upside down at that time — that is wrong view at that time. ^1-381

382. What is wrong intention at that time? Putting the mind onto something in a wrong way at that time — that is wrong intention at that time. ^1-382

383. What is wrong effort at that time? The effort that strives in a wrong way at that time — that is wrong effort at that time. ^1-383

384. What is wrong calm focus at that time? The mind staying on one thing in a wrong way at that time — that is wrong calm focus at that time. ^1-384

385. What is the strength of effort at that time? The effort at that time — that is the strength of effort at that time. ^1-385

386. What is the strength of calm focus at that time? The mind staying on one thing at that time — that is the strength of calm focus at that time. ^1-386

387. What is the strength of not feeling conscience at that time? Not feeling conscience when one should, about things bad for the mind — that is the strength of not feeling conscience at that time. ^1-387

388. What is the strength of not worrying about wrong action at that time? Not worrying when one should, about things bad for the mind — that is the strength of not worrying about wrong action at that time. ^1-388

389. What is greed at that time? The greed, being greedy, state of being greedy, being stained with desire, staining with desire, state of being stained with desire, coveting, greed — a root of what is bad for the mind — that is greed at that time. ^1-389

390. What is delusion at that time? Not knowing, not seeing, not fully grasping, not understanding deeply, not waking up to, not penetrating, not encompassing, not examining all sides, not looking at, not looking back, mental dullness, foolishness, not knowing clearly what one is doing, delusion, being fooled, being very fooled, not knowing, flood of not knowing, yoke of not knowing, underlying tendency of not knowing, active state of not knowing, stain of not knowing, delusion — a root of what is bad for the mind — that is delusion at that time. ^1-390

391. What is coveting at that time? Greed at that time — that is coveting at that time. ^1-391

392. What is wrong view at that time? The wrong view and wrong fixed idea at that time — that is wrong view at that time. ^1-392

393. What is not feeling conscience at that time? Not feeling conscience when one should, about things bad for the mind — that is not feeling conscience at that time. ^1-393

394. What is not worrying about wrong action at that time? Not worrying when one should, about things bad for the mind — that is not worrying about wrong action at that time. ^1-394

395. What is calmness at that time? The mind staying on one thing without wandering at that time — that is calmness at that time. ^1-395

396. What is striving at that time? The effort at that time — that is striving at that time. ^1-396

397. What is freedom from distraction at that time? The mind staying on one thing without scattering at that time — that is freedom from distraction at that time; and whatever other formless things that arise from causes are present at that time are bad for the mind.
At that time there are four aggregates, two sense bases, two elements, three foods, five powers, five-part deep calm, four-part path, four strengths, two causes, one contact … one mental-object sense base, one mental-object element; and whatever other formless things that arise from causes are present at that time — these things are bad for the mind … ^1-397

398. What is the aggregate of habits and intentions at that time? #### Indeterminate Resultant ^1-1-7-0

##### Wholesome Resultant Five Sense Consciousnesses ^1-1-7-1-0

431. What things are neither good nor bad for the mind? At a time when wholesome action of the sense-desire level has been done and accumulated, resultant eye-consciousness arises, accompanied by calm balance, focused on a visible form — at that time there is contact, feeling, perception, intention, mind, calm balance, one-pointedness of mind, power of the mind, power of equipoise, power of life, and whatever other formless things that arise from causes are present at that time — these things are neither good nor bad for the mind. ^1-431

432. What is contact at that time? The contact at that time that is touching, contacting, and the state of being contacted — that is contact at that time. ^1-432

433. What is feeling at that time? The neutral mental feeling born of eye-contact at that time, neither pleasant nor painful — that is feeling at that time. ^1-433

434. What is perception at that time? The perceiving and state of having perceived born of eye-contact at that time — that is perception at that time. ^1-434

435. What is intention at that time? The intending and state of having intended born of eye-contact at that time — that is intention at that time. ^1-435

436. What is mind at that time? The mind, heart, and consciousness that is the eye-consciousness element at that time — that is mind at that time. ^1-436

437. What is calm balance at that time? The neutral mental feeling born of eye-contact at that time, neither pleasant nor painful — that is calm balance at that time. ^1-437

438. What is one-pointedness of mind at that time? The steadiness of the mind at that time — that is one-pointedness of mind at that time. ^1-438

439. What is the power of the mind at that time? The mind that knows at that time — that is the power of the mind at that time. ^1-439

440. What is the power of equipoise at that time? The neutral mental feeling born of eye-contact at that time, neither pleasant nor painful — that is the power of equipoise at that time. ^1-440

441. What is the power of life at that time? The life that sustains those formless things at that time — that is the power of life at that time; and whatever other formless things that arise from causes are present at that time are neither good nor bad for the mind.
At that time there are four aggregates, two sense bases, two elements, three foods, three powers, one contact … one eye-consciousness element, one mental-object sense base, one mental-object element; and whatever other formless things that arise from causes are present at that time — these things are neither good nor bad for the mind … ^1-441

472. What is perception at that time? The perceiving and state of having perceived born of mind-contact at that time — that is perception at that time. ^1-472

473. What is intention at that time? The intending and state of having intended born of mind-contact at that time — that is intention at that time. ^1-473

474. What is mind at that time? The mind, heart, and consciousness that is the mind-consciousness element at that time — that is mind at that time. ^1-474

475. What is putting the mind onto something at that time? The placing and directing of the mind at that time — that is putting the mind onto something at that time. ^1-475

476. What is keeping the mind on something at that time? The following along, exploring, and looking over of the mind at that time — that is keeping the mind on something at that time. ^1-476

477. What is a feeling of joy at that time? The joy, gladness, delight, and lightness in the mind at that time — that is a feeling of joy at that time. ^1-477

478. What is comfort at that time? The pleasant mental comfort and happy feeling born of mind-contact at that time — that is comfort at that time. ^1-478

479. What is one-pointedness of mind at that time? The steadiness of the mind at that time — that is one-pointedness of mind at that time. ^1-479

480. What is the power of the mind at that time? The mind that knows at that time — that is the power of the mind at that time. ^1-480

481. What is the power of happy feeling at that time? The pleasant mental comfort in the mind at that time — that is the power of happy feeling at that time. ^1-481

482. What is the power of life at that time? The life that sustains those formless things at that time — that is the power of life at that time; and whatever other formless things that arise from causes are present at that time are neither good nor bad for the mind.
At that time there are four aggregates, two sense bases, two elements, three foods, three powers, one contact … one mind-consciousness element, one mental-object sense base, one mental-object element; and whatever other formless things that arise from causes are present at that time — these things are neither good nor bad for the mind … ^1-482

483. What is the aggregate of habits and intentions at that time? Contact, intention, putting the mind onto something, keeping the mind on something, a feeling of joy, one-pointedness of mind, power of life, and whatever other formless things that arise from causes are present at that time, except the aggregate of feeling, except the aggregate of perception, except the aggregate of consciousness — that is the aggregate of habits and intentions at that time … these things are neither good nor bad for the mind.
Wholesome Resultant Mind-Consciousness Element Accompanied by Happy Mental Feeling. ^1-483

##### Wholesome Resultant Mind-Consciousness Element Accompanied by Calm Balance ^1-1-7-4-0

484. What things are neither good nor bad for the mind? At a time when wholesome action of the sense-desire level has been done and accumulated, resultant mind-consciousness element arises, accompanied by calm balance, focused on a visible form … or on a mental object, or on whatever it may be based — at that time there is contact, feeling, perception, intention, mind, putting the mind onto something, keeping the mind on something, calm balance, one-pointedness of mind, power of the mind, power of equipoise, power of life, and whatever other formless things that arise from causes are present at that time — these things are neither good nor bad for the mind. ^1-484

485. What is contact at that time? The contact at that time that is touching, contacting, and the state of being contacted — that is contact at that time. ^1-485

486. What is feeling at that time? The neutral mental feeling born of mind-contact at that time, neither pleasant nor painful — that is feeling at that time. ^1-486

487. What is perception at that time? The perceiving and state of having perceived born of mind-contact at that time — that is perception at that time. ^1-487

488. What is intention at that time? The intending and state of having intended born of mind-contact at that time — that is intention at that time. ^1-488

489. What is mind at that time? The mind, heart, and consciousness that is the mind-consciousness element at that time — that is mind at that time. ^1-489

490. What is putting the mind onto something at that time? The placing and directing of the mind at that time — that is putting the mind onto something at that time. ^1-490

491. What is keeping the mind on something at that time? The following along, exploring, and looking over of the mind at that time — that is keeping the mind on something at that time. ^1-491

492. What is calm balance at that time? The neutral mental feeling born of mind-contact at that time, neither pleasant nor painful — that is calm balance at that time. ^1-492

493. What is one-pointedness of mind at that time? The steadiness of the mind at that time — that is one-pointedness of mind at that time. ^1-493

494. What is the power of the mind at that time? The mind that knows at that time — that is the power of the mind at that time. ^1-494

495. What is the power of equipoise at that time? The neutral mental feeling born of mind-contact at that time, neither pleasant nor painful — that is the power of equipoise at that time. ^1-495

496. What is the power of life at that time? The life that sustains those formless things at that time — that is the power of life at that time; and whatever other formless things that arise from causes are present at that time are neither good nor bad for the mind.
At that time there are four aggregates, two sense bases, two elements, three foods, three powers, one contact … one mind-consciousness element, one mental-object sense base, one mental-object element; and whatever other formless things that arise from causes are present at that time — these things are neither good nor bad for the mind … ^1-496

497. What is the aggregate of habits and intentions at that time? Contact, intention, putting the mind onto something, keeping the mind on something, one-pointedness of mind, power of life, and whatever other formless things that arise from causes are present at that time, except the aggregate of feeling, except the aggregate of perception, except the aggregate of consciousness — that is the aggregate of habits and intentions at that time … these things are neither good nor bad for the mind.
Wholesome Resultant Mind-Consciousness Element Accompanied by Calm Balance. ^1-497

##### Eight Great Resultants ^1-1-7-5-0

498. What things are neither good nor bad for the mind? At a time when wholesome action of the sense-desire level has been done and accumulated, resultant mind-consciousness element arises, accompanied by a happy mental feeling and associated with knowledge … accompanied by a happy mental feeling and associated with knowledge, with conditioning … accompanied by a happy mental feeling and separated from knowledge … accompanied by a happy mental feeling and separated from knowledge, with conditioning … accompanied by calm balance and associated with knowledge … accompanied by calm balance and associated with knowledge, with conditioning … accompanied by calm balance and separated from knowledge … accompanied by calm balance and separated from knowledge, with conditioning, focused on a visible form … or on a mental object, or on whatever it may be based — at that time there is contact … freedom from distraction … these things are neither good nor bad for the mind … non-greed — a root of what is neither good nor bad for the mind … non-hatred — a root of what is neither good nor bad for the mind … these things are neither good nor bad for the mind.
Eight Great Resultants. ^1-498

##### Form-Level Resultants ^1-1-7-6-0

At a time when one develops the path for rebirth in the form level, fully separated from sense desire … reaching and dwelling in the first state of deep calm in meditation on the earth meditation object — at that time there is contact … freedom from distraction … these things are good for the mind. When that wholesome action of the form level has been done and accumulated, resultant, fully separated from sense desire … reaching and dwelling in the first state of deep calm in meditation on the earth meditation object — at that time there is contact … freedom from distraction … these things are neither good nor bad for the mind. ^1-499

When that wholesome action of the form level has been done and accumulated, resultant, with the giving up of comfort … reaching the fifth state of deep calm in meditation on the earth meditation object — at that time there is contact … freedom from distraction … these things are neither good nor bad for the mind.

##### Second and Further Path Resultants ^1-1-8-9-0

553. What things are neither good nor bad for the mind? At a time when one develops supramundane deep calm, leading out, tending to the decline of defilements, for the thinning of sense desire and ill-will, for attainment of the second stage … for the complete abandoning of sense desire and ill-will, for attainment of the third stage … for the complete abandoning of desire for form, desire for the formless, pride, and not knowing, for attainment of the fourth stage, fully separated from sense desire … reaching and dwelling in the first state of deep calm in meditation by the painful way of practice with slow understanding — at that time there is contact … power of one who will fully know … freedom from distraction … these things are good for the mind. When that supramundane wholesome deep calm has been done and developed, resultant, fully separated from sense desire … reaching and dwelling in the first state of deep calm in meditation by the painful way of practice with slow understanding in emptiness — at that time there is contact … power of one who has fully known … freedom from distraction … and whatever other formless things that arise from causes are present at that time — these things are neither good nor bad for the mind. ^1-553

554. What is contact at that time? The contact at that time that is touching, contacting, and the state of being contacted — that is contact at that time … ^1-554

555. What is the power of one who has fully known at that time? The wisdom of those who have fully known, understanding, examining closely, investigation of things, right view, the factor of awakening for investigation of things, a path factor included in the path — that is the power of one who has fully known at that time … and whatever other formless things that arise from causes are present at that time are neither good nor bad for the mind.
Second and Further Path Resultants.
Supramundane Resultant. ^1-555

#### Unwholesome Resultant Indeterminate ^1-1-9-0

##### Unwholesome Resultant Five Sense Consciousnesses ^1-1-9-1-0

556. What things are neither good nor bad for the mind? At a time when unwholesome action has been done and accumulated, resultant eye-consciousness arises, accompanied by calm balance, focused on a visible form … ear-consciousness arises, accompanied by calm balance, focused on a sound … nose-consciousness arises, accompanied by calm balance, focused on a smell … tongue-consciousness arises, accompanied by calm balance, focused on a taste … body-consciousness arises, accompanied by pain, focused on a touch — at that time there is contact, feeling, perception, intention, mind, pain, one-pointedness of mind, power of the mind, power of pain, power of life, and whatever other formless things that arise from causes are present at that time — these things are neither good nor bad for the mind. ^1-556

557. What is contact at that time? The contact at that time that is touching, contacting, and the state of being contacted — that is contact at that time. ^1-557

558. What is feeling at that time? The unpleasant bodily pain and painful feeling born of body-contact at that time — that is feeling at that time … ^1-558

559. What is pain at that time? The unpleasant bodily pain and painful feeling born of body-contact at that time — that is pain at that time … ^1-559

560. What is the power of pain at that time? The unpleasant bodily pain and painful feeling born of body-contact at that time — that is the power of pain at that time … and whatever other formless things that arise from causes are present at that time are neither good nor bad for the mind.
At that time there are four aggregates, two sense bases, two elements, three foods, three powers, one contact … one body-consciousness element, one mental-object sense base, one mental-object element; and whatever other formless things that arise from causes are present at that time — these things are neither good nor bad for the mind … ^1-560

561. What is the aggregate of habits and intentions at that time? Contact, intention, one-pointedness of mind, power of life, and whatever other formless things that arise from causes are present at that time, except the aggregate of feeling, except the aggregate of perception, except the aggregate of consciousness — that is the aggregate of habits and intentions at that time … these things are neither good nor bad for the mind.
Unwholesome Resultant Five Sense Consciousnesses. ^1-561

##### Unwholesome Resultant Mind Element ^1-1-9-2-0

562. What things are neither good nor bad for the mind? At a time when unwholesome action has been done and accumulated, resultant mind element arises, accompanied by calm balance, focused on a visible form … or on a touch, or on whatever it may be based — at that time there is contact, feeling, perception, intention, mind, putting the mind onto something, keeping the mind on something, calm balance, one-pointedness of mind, power of the mind, power of equipoise, power of life, and whatever other formless things that arise from causes are present at that time — these things are neither good nor bad for the mind …
At that time there are four aggregates, two sense bases, two elements, three foods, three powers, one contact … one mind element, one mental-object sense base, one mental-object element; and whatever other formless things that arise from causes are present at that time — these things are neither good nor bad for the mind … ^1-562

563. What is the aggregate of habits and intentions at that time? Contact, intention, putting the mind onto something, keeping the mind on something, one-pointedness of mind, power of life, and whatever other formless things that arise from causes are present at that time, except the aggregate of feeling, except the aggregate of perception, except the aggregate of consciousness — that is the aggregate of habits and intentions at that time … these things are neither good nor bad for the mind.
Unwholesome Resultant Mind Element. ^1-563

##### Functional Mind-Consciousness Element Accompanied by Happy Mental Feeling ^1-1-10-2-0

At a time when functional mind-consciousness element arises — neither wholesome nor unwholesome nor result of action — accompanied by a happy mental feeling, focused on a visible form … or on a mental object, or on whatever it may be based — at that time there is contact, feeling, perception, intention, mind, putting the mind onto something, keeping the mind on something, a feeling of joy, comfort, one-pointedness of mind, power of effort, power of calm focus, power of the mind, power of happy feeling, power of life, and whatever other formless things that arise from causes are present at that time — these things are neither good nor bad for the mind. ^1-568

570. What is one-pointedness of mind at that time? The steadiness, settling, and calm focus of the mind without distraction at that time — that is one-pointedness of mind at that time. ^1-570

571. What is the power of effort at that time? The starting, striving, and not giving up in the mind at that time — that is the power of effort at that time. ^1-571

572. What is the power of calm focus at that time? The steadiness and settling of the mind without distraction at that time — that is the power of calm focus at that time … and whatever other formless things that arise from causes are present at that time are neither good nor bad for the mind.

Rootless Functional Indeterminate. ^1-575

##### With-Cause Sense-Desire Functional ^1-1-10-4-0

576. What things are neither good nor bad for the mind? At a time when functional mind-consciousness element arises — neither wholesome nor unwholesome nor result of action — accompanied by a happy mental feeling and associated with knowledge … accompanied by a happy mental feeling and associated with knowledge, with conditioning … accompanied by a happy mental feeling and separated from knowledge … accompanied by a happy mental feeling and separated from knowledge, with conditioning … accompanied by calm balance and associated with knowledge … accompanied by calm balance and associated with knowledge, with conditioning … accompanied by calm balance and separated from knowledge … accompanied by calm balance and separated from knowledge, with conditioning, focused on a visible form … or on a mental object, or on whatever it may be based — at that time there is contact … freedom from distraction … these things are neither good nor bad for the mind … non-greed — a root of what is neither good nor bad for the mind … non-hatred — a root of what is neither good nor bad for the mind … these things are neither good nor bad for the mind.
With-Cause Sense-Desire Functional. ^1-576

##### Form-Level Functional ^1-1-10-5-0

577. What things are neither good nor bad for the mind? At a time when one develops form-level functional deep calm — neither wholesome nor unwholesome nor result of action — for dwelling in comfort in this very life, fully separated from sense desire … reaching and dwelling in the first state of deep calm in meditation on the earth meditation object — at that time there is contact … freedom from distraction … these things are neither good nor bad for the mind. ^1-577

578. What things are neither good nor bad for the mind? At a time when one develops form-level functional deep calm — neither wholesome nor unwholesome nor result of action — for dwelling in comfort in this very life, with the calming of putting the mind onto something and keeping the mind on something … reaching the second … third … fourth … first … fifth state of deep calm in meditation on the earth meditation object — at that time there is contact … freedom from distraction … these things are neither good nor bad for the mind.
Form-Level Functional. ^1-578

##### Formless-Level Functional ^1-1-10-6-0

579. What things are neither good nor bad for the mind? At a time when one develops formless-level functional deep calm — neither wholesome nor unwholesome nor result of action — for dwelling in comfort in this very life, having fully gone beyond all perceptions of form … accompanied by the perception of the base of infinite space, with the giving up of comfort … reaching the fourth state of deep calm in meditation — at that time there is contact … freedom from distraction … these things are neither good nor bad for the mind. ^1-579

580. What things are neither good nor bad for the mind? At a time when one develops formless-level functional deep calm — neither wholesome nor unwholesome nor result of action — for dwelling in comfort in this very life, having fully gone beyond the base of infinite space, accompanied by the perception of the base of infinite consciousness, with the giving up of comfort … reaching the fourth state of deep calm in meditation — at that time there is contact … freedom from distraction … these things are neither good nor bad for the mind. ^1-580

581. What things are neither good nor bad for the mind? At a time when one develops formless-level functional deep calm — neither wholesome nor unwholesome nor result of action — for dwelling in comfort in this very life, having fully gone beyond the base of infinite consciousness, accompanied by the perception of the base of nothingness, with the giving up of comfort … reaching the fourth state of deep calm in meditation — at that time there is contact … freedom from distraction … these things are neither good nor bad for the mind. ^1-581

582. What things are neither good nor bad for the mind? At a time when one develops formless-level functional deep calm — neither wholesome nor unwholesome nor result of action — for dwelling in comfort in this very life, having fully gone beyond the base of nothingness, accompanied by the perception of the base of neither perception nor non-perception, with the giving up of comfort … reaching the fourth state of deep calm in meditation — at that time there is contact … freedom from distraction … these things are neither good nor bad for the mind … non-greed — a root of what is neither good nor bad for the mind … non-hatred — a root of what is neither good nor bad for the mind … wisdom without delusion — a root of what is neither good nor bad for the mind … these things are neither good nor bad for the mind.
Formless-Level Functional.
Functional Indeterminate.
Section on Minds That Arise. ^1-582

### Section on Form ^1-2-0

#### Introduction ^1-2-1-0

583. What things are neither good nor bad for the mind? Results of wholesome and unwholesome things of the sense-desire level, form level, and formless level, not included in the aggregates — the aggregate of feeling, the aggregate of perception, the aggregate of habits and intentions, the aggregate of consciousness; and things that are functional — neither wholesome nor unwholesome nor result of action; and all form; and the unconditioned element — these things are neither good nor bad for the mind. ^1-583

#### Mātikā ^1-2-2-0

##### Single Items ^1-2-2-1-0

584. What is all form? The four great elements and form derived from the four great elements — this is called all form. All form is not a cause, is without cause, is separated from cause, has supporting conditions, is made, is form, is worldly, has mental stains, is something that can tie, is something that can clutch, is something that can flood, is something that can yoke, is something that can block the mind, is something that can be clutched, is something that can be taken as true, is something that can be clung to, stains the mind, is neither good nor bad for the mind, has no object, is not a quality of the mind, is separated from mind, is neither resultant nor what produces result, is not what stains and is not what is stained, is not accompanied by putting the mind onto something and keeping the mind on something, is not only keeping the mind on something, is without putting the mind onto something and without keeping the mind on something, is not accompanied by a feeling of joy, is not accompanied by comfort, is not accompanied by calm balance, is not to be abandoned by seeing nor by development, is not a cause to be abandoned by seeing nor by development, is not what increases defilements nor what decreases defilements, is neither what belongs to one in training nor what does not belong to one in training, is small, belongs to the sense-desire level, does not belong to the form level, does not belong to the formless level, is included in the aggregates, is not not included in the aggregates, is not fixed, does not lead out, has arisen, is known by six kinds of consciousness, is impermanent, is overcome by aging.
Thus the collection of form in one way.
Single Items. ^1-584

##### Pairs ^1-2-2-2-0

Collection of form in two ways —
There is form that is derived, and form that is not derived.
There is form that is taken up, and form that is not taken up.
There is form that is taken up and can be clung to, and form that is not taken up and cannot be clung to.
There is form that can be seen, and form that cannot be seen.
There is form that can be pushed against, and form that cannot be pushed against.
There is form that is a power, and form that is not a power.
There is form that is a great element, and form that is not a great element.
There is form that is communication, and form that is not communication.
There is form produced by mind, and form not produced by mind.
There is form that arises together with mind, and form that does not arise together with mind.
There is form that follows mind, and form that does not follow mind.
There is form that is internal, and form that is external.
There is form that is coarse, and form that is fine.
There is form that is far, and form that is near.
There is form that is the basis for eye-contact, and form that is not the basis for eye-contact. There is form that is the basis for feeling … perception … intention … eye-consciousness born of eye-contact, and form that is not the basis for eye-consciousness.
There is form that is the basis for ear-contact … nose-contact … tongue-contact … body-contact, and form that is not the basis for body-contact. There is form that is the basis for feeling … perception … intention … body-consciousness born of body-contact, and form that is not the basis for body-consciousness.
There is form that is the object of eye-contact, and form that is not the object of eye-contact. There is form that is the object of feeling … perception … intention … eye-consciousness born of eye-contact, and form that is not the object of eye-consciousness.
There is form that is the object of ear-contact … nose-contact … tongue-contact … body-contact, and form that is not the object of body-contact. There is form that is the object of feeling … perception … intention … body-consciousness born of body-contact, and form that is not the object of body-consciousness.
There is form that is the eye sense base, and form that is not the eye sense base. There is form that is the ear sense base … nose sense base … tongue sense base … body sense base, and form that is not the body sense base.
There is form that is the visible-form sense base, and form that is not the visible-form sense base. There is form that is the sound sense base … smell sense base … taste sense base … touch sense base, and form that is not the touch sense base.
There is form that is the eye element, and form that is not the eye element. There is form that is the ear element … nose element … tongue element … body element, and form that is not the body element.
There is form that is the visible-form element, and form that is not the visible-form element. There is form that is the sound element … smell element … taste element … touch element, and form that is not the touch element.
There is form that is the eye power, and form that is not the eye power. There is form that is the ear power … nose power … tongue power … body power, and form that is not the body power.
There is form that is the female power, and form that is not the female power.
There is form that is the male power, and form that is not the male power.
There is form that is the life power, and form that is not the life power.
There is form that is bodily communication, and form that is not bodily communication.
There is form that is verbal communication, and form that is not verbal communication.
There is form that is the space element, and form that is not the space element.
There is form that is the water element, and form that is not the water element.
There is lightness of form, and form that is not lightness.
There is softness of form, and form that is not softness.
There is readiness of form, and form that is not readiness.
There is growth of form, and form that is not growth.
There is continuity of form, and form that is not continuity.
There is aging of form, and form that is not aging.
There is impermanence of form, and form that is not impermanence.
There is form that is physical food, and form that is not physical food.
Thus the collection of form in two ways.
Pairs.

That form which is a great element — some is far, and some is near. That form which is not a great element — some is far, and some is near.

##### Dual Analysis ^1-2-3-2-0

##### Analysis of Derived Form ^1-2-3-3-0

595. What form is derived? Eye sense base, ear sense base, nose sense base, tongue sense base, body sense base, visible-form sense base, sound sense base, smell sense base, taste sense base, female power, male power, life power, bodily communication, verbal communication, space element, lightness of form, softness of form, readiness of form, growth of form, continuity of form, aging of form, impermanence of form, physical food. ^1-595

596. What is the eye sense base? The sensitivity derived from the four great elements, included in the person, cannot be seen, can be pushed against, by which the eye — cannot be seen, can be pushed against — sees visible form that can be seen and can be pushed against, or has seen, sees, will see, or might see — that eye, that eye sense base, that eye element, that eye power, that world, that door, that ocean, that white, that field, that base, that net, that eye, that seeing, that near shore, that empty village — that is the eye sense base. ^1-596

597. What is the eye sense base? The sensitivity derived from the four great elements, included in the person, cannot be seen, can be pushed against, in which the eye — cannot be seen, can be pushed against — visible form that can be seen and can be pushed against is pushed against, is being pushed against, will be pushed against, or might be pushed against — that eye, that eye sense base, that eye element, that eye power, that world, that door, that ocean, that white, that field, that base, that net, that eye, that seeing, that near shore, that empty village — that is the eye sense base. ^1-597

599. What is the eye sense base?
