1441. What things are causes? Three wholesome causes, three unwholesome causes, three indeterminate causes. Non-greed is a wholesome cause, non-hatred is a wholesome cause — they arise in wholesome in four levels. Wisdom without delusion is a wholesome cause — except four mind-arisings separated from knowledge from sense-desire-level wholesome, it arises in wholesome in four levels.
Greed arises in eight mind-arisings accompanied by greed. Hatred arises in two mind-arisings accompanied by dejection. Delusion arises in all unwholesome.
Non-greed is a result-cause, non-hatred is a result-cause — except rootless mind-arisings from result of sense-desire level, they arise in results in four levels. Wisdom without delusion is a result-cause — except rootless mind-arisings from result of sense-desire level and four mind-arisings separated from knowledge, it arises in results in four levels.
Non-greed is a functional-cause, non-hatred is a functional-cause — except rootless mind-arisings from sense-desire-level functional, they arise in functional states in three levels. Wisdom without delusion is a functional-cause — except rootless mind-arisings from sense-desire-level functional and four mind-arisings separated from knowledge, it arises in functional states in three levels — these things are causes. ^1-1441

1442. What things are not causes? Except causes, wholesome and unwholesome in four levels, result in four levels, functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, form, and nibbana — these things are not causes. ^1-1442

1443. What things have causes? Except delusion accompanied by doubt and delusion accompanied by restlessness, the remaining unwholesome, wholesome in four levels, except rootless mind-arisings from result of sense-desire level result in four levels, except rootless mind-arisings from sense-desire-level functional functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels — these things have causes. ^1-1443

1444. What things have no causes? Delusion accompanied by doubt, delusion accompanied by restlessness, the two sets of five consciousnesses, the three mind elements, five rootless mind-consciousness elements, form, and nibbana — these things have no causes. ^1-1444

1445. What things are united with causes? Except delusion accompanied by doubt and delusion accompanied by restlessness, the remaining unwholesome, wholesome in four levels, except rootless mind-arisings from result of sense-desire level result in four levels, except rootless mind-arisings from sense-desire-level functional functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels — these things are united with causes. ^1-1445

1446. What things are separated from causes? Delusion accompanied by doubt, delusion accompanied by restlessness, the two sets of five consciousnesses, the three mind elements, five rootless mind-consciousness elements, form, and nibbana — these things are separated from causes. ^1-1446

1447. What things are both causes and have causes? Where two or three causes arise together — these things are both causes and have causes. ^1-1447

1448. What things have causes but are not causes? Wholesome and unwholesome in four levels, except rootless mind-arisings from result of sense-desire level result in four levels, except rootless mind-arisings from sense-desire-level functional functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, except causes that arise therein — these things have causes but are not causes. Things with no causes should not be said to be both causes and have causes, nor have causes but not be causes. ^1-1448

1449. What things are both causes and united with causes? Where two or three causes arise together — these things are both causes and united with causes. ^1-1449

1450. What things are united with causes but are not causes? Wholesome and unwholesome in four levels, except rootless mind-arisings from result of sense-desire level result in four levels, except rootless mind-arisings from sense-desire-level functional functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, except causes that arise therein — these things are united with causes but are not causes. Things separated from causes should not be said to be both causes and united with causes, nor united with causes but not causes. ^1-1450

1451. What things are not causes but have causes? Wholesome and unwholesome in four levels, except rootless mind-arisings from result of sense-desire level result in four levels, except rootless mind-arisings from sense-desire-level functional functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, except causes that arise therein — these things are not causes but have causes. ^1-1451

1452. What things are not causes and have no causes? The two sets of five consciousnesses, the three mind elements, five rootless mind-consciousness elements, form, and nibbana — these things are not causes and have no causes. Causes should not be said to be not causes but have causes, nor not causes and have no causes. ^1-1452

##### Short Interior Duals ^1-4-2-2-0

1453. What things have supporting conditions? Wholesome and unwholesome in four levels, result in four levels, functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, and all form — these things have supporting conditions. ^1-1453

1454. What things have no supporting conditions? Nibbana — these things have no supporting conditions. ^1-1454

1455. What things are made? Wholesome and unwholesome in four levels, result in four levels, functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, and all form — these things are made. ^1-1455

1456. What things are unmade? Nibbana — these things are unmade. ^1-1456

1457. What things can be seen? Visible-form sense base — these things can be seen. ^1-1457

1458. What things cannot be seen? Eye sense base … touch sense base, wholesome and unwholesome in four levels, result in four levels, functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, whatever form cannot be seen and cannot be pushed against that is included in the mental-object sense base, and nibbana — these things cannot be seen. ^1-1458

1459. What things can be pushed against? Eye sense base … touch sense base — these things can be pushed against. ^1-1459

1460. What things cannot be pushed against? Wholesome and unwholesome in four levels, result in four levels, functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, whatever form cannot be seen and cannot be pushed against that is included in the mental-object sense base, and nibbana — these things cannot be pushed against. ^1-1460

1461. What things have form? The four great elements and form derived from the four great elements — these things have form. ^1-1461

1462. What things are formless? Wholesome and unwholesome in four levels, result in four levels, functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, and nibbana — these things are formless. ^1-1462

1463. What things are worldly? Wholesome and unwholesome in three levels, result in three levels, functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, and all form — these things are worldly. ^1-1463

1464. What things are supramundane? The four paths that are not included, the four noble-fruition results, and nibbana — these things are supramundane. All things are knowable by some and not knowable by some. ^1-1464

##### Floods Group ^1-4-2-3-0

1465. What things are floods? The four floods — sense-desire flood, becoming flood, view flood, ignorance flood. The sense-desire flood arises in eight mind-arisings accompanied by greed. The becoming flood arises in four mind-arisings accompanied by greed, separated from wrong view. The view flood arises in four mind-arisings united with wrong view. The ignorance flood arises in all unwholesome — these things are floods. ^1-1465

1466. What things are not floods? Except floods, the remaining unwholesome, wholesome in four levels, result in four levels, functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, form, and nibbana — these things are not floods. ^1-1466

1467. What things have mental stains? Wholesome and unwholesome in three levels, result in three levels, functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, and all form — these things have mental stains. ^1-1467

1468. What things have no mental stains? The four paths that are not included, the four noble-fruition results, and nibbana — these things have no mental stains. ^1-1468

1469. What things are united with floods? Two mind-arisings accompanied by dejection, except delusion that arises therein; except delusion accompanied by doubt and delusion accompanied by restlessness; the remaining unwholesome — these things are united with floods. ^1-1469

1470. What things are separated from floods? Delusion that has arisen in two mind-arisings accompanied by dejection, delusion accompanied by doubt, delusion accompanied by restlessness, wholesome in four levels, result in four levels, functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, form, and nibbana — these things are separated from floods. ^1-1470

1471. What things are both floods and have mental stains? Those very floods are both floods and have mental stains. ^1-1471

1472. What things have mental stains but are not floods? Except floods, the remaining unwholesome, wholesome in three levels, result in three levels, functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, and all form — these things have mental stains but are not floods. Things with no mental stains should not be said to be both floods and have mental stains, nor have mental stains but not be floods. ^1-1472

1473. What things are both floods and united with floods? Where two or three floods arise together — these things are both floods and united with floods. ^1-1473

1474. What things are united with floods but are not floods? Except floods, the remaining unwholesome — these things are united with floods but are not floods. Things separated from floods should not be said to be both floods and united with floods, nor united with floods but not floods. ^1-1474

1475. What things are separated from floods and have mental stains? Delusion that has arisen in two mind-arisings accompanied by dejection, delusion accompanied by doubt, delusion accompanied by restlessness, wholesome in three levels, result in three levels, functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, and all form — these things are separated from floods and have mental stains. ^1-1475

1476. What things are separated from floods and have no mental stains? The four paths that are not included, the four noble-fruition results, and nibbana — these things are separated from floods and have no mental stains. Things united with floods should not be said to be separated from floods and have mental stains, nor separated from floods and have no mental stains. ^1-1476

##### Fetters Group ^1-4-2-4-0

1477. What things are fetters? The ten fetters — fetter of lust for sense pleasures, fetter of repulsion, fetter of pride, fetter of views, fetter of doubt, fetter of misbelief in rites and vows, fetter of lust for becoming, fetter of jealousy, fetter of stinginess, fetter of ignorance. The fetter of lust for sense pleasures arises in eight mind-arisings accompanied by greed. The fetter of repulsion arises in two mind-arisings accompanied by dejection. The fetter of pride arises in four mind-arisings accompanied by greed, separated from wrong view. The fetter of views arises in four mind-arisings united with wrong view. The fetter of doubt arises in mind-arisings accompanied by doubt. The fetter of misbelief in rites and vows arises in four mind-arisings united with wrong view. The fetter of lust for becoming arises in four mind-arisings accompanied by greed, separated from wrong view. The fetter of jealousy and the fetter of stinginess arise in two mind-arisings accompanied by dejection. The fetter of ignorance arises in all unwholesome — these things are fetters. ^1-1477

1478. What things are not fetters. Except fetters, the remaining unwholesome, wholesome in four levels, result in four levels, functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, form, and nibbana — these things are not fetters. ^1-1478

1479. What things are fetterable? Wholesome and unwholesome in three levels, result in three levels, functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, and all form — these things are fetterable. ^1-1479

1480. What things are not fetterable? The four paths that are not included, the four noble-fruition results, and nibbana — these things are not fetterable. ^1-1480

1481. What things are united with fetters? Except delusion accompanied by restlessness, the remaining unwholesome — these things are united with fetters. ^1-1481

1482. What things are separated from fetters? Delusion accompanied by restlessness, wholesome in four levels, result in four levels, functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, form, and nibbana — these things are separated from fetters. ^1-1482

1483. What things are both fetters and fetterable? Those very fetters are both fetters and fetterable. ^1-1483

1484. What things are fetterable but are not fetters? Except fetters, the remaining unwholesome, wholesome in three levels, result in three levels, functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, and all form — these things are fetterable but are not fetters. Things not fetterable should not be said to be both fetters and fetterable, nor fetterable but not fetters. ^1-1484

1485. What things are both fetters and united with fetters? Where two or three fetters arise together — these things are both fetters and united with fetters. ^1-1485

1486. What things are united with fetters but are not fetters? Except fetters, the remaining unwholesome — these things are united with fetters but are not fetters. Things separated from fetters should not be said to be both fetters and united with fetters, nor united with fetters but not fetters. ^1-1486

1487. What things are separated from fetters and fetterable? Delusion accompanied by restlessness, wholesome in three levels, result in three levels, functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, and all form — these things are separated from fetters and fetterable. ^1-1487

1488. What things are separated from fetters and not fetterable? The four paths that are not included, the four noble-fruition results, and nibbana — these things are separated from fetters and not fetterable. Things united with fetters should not be said to be separated from fetters and fetterable, nor separated from fetters and not fetterable. ^1-1488

##### Bonds Group ^1-4-2-5-0

1489. What things are bonds? The four bonds — bond of coveting, bond of ill will, bond of misbelief in rites and vows, bond of clinging to this alone as truth. The bond of coveting arises in eight mind-arisings accompanied by greed. The bond of ill will arises in two mind-arisings accompanied by dejection. The bond of misbelief in rites and vows and the bond of clinging to this alone as truth arise in four mind-arisings united with wrong view — these things are bonds. ^1-1489

1490. What things are not bonds? Except bonds, the remaining unwholesome, wholesome in four levels, result in four levels, functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, form, and nibbana — these things are not bonds. ^1-1490

1491. What things are bondable? Wholesome and unwholesome in three levels, result in three levels, functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, and all form — these things are bondable. ^1-1491

1492. What things are not bondable? The four paths that are not included, the four noble-fruition results, and nibbana — these things are not bondable. ^1-1492

1493. What things are united with bonds? Four mind-arisings united with wrong view, four mind-arisings accompanied by greed, separated from wrong view, except greed that arises therein, two mind-arisings accompanied by dejection, except repulsion that arises therein — these things are united with bonds. ^1-1493

1494. What things are separated from bonds? Greed that has arisen in four mind-arisings accompanied by greed, separated from wrong view, repulsion that has arisen in two mind-arisings accompanied by dejection, mind-arising accompanied by doubt, mind-arising accompanied by restlessness, wholesome in four levels, result in four levels, functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, form, and nibbana — these things are separated from bonds. ^1-1494

1495. What things are both bonds and bondable? Those very bonds are both bonds and bondable. ^1-1495

1496. What things are bondable but are not bonds? Except bonds, the remaining unwholesome, wholesome in three levels, result in three levels, functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, and all form — these things are bondable but are not bonds. Things not bondable should not be said to be both bonds and bondable, nor bondable but not bonds. ^1-1496

1497. What things are both bonds and united with bonds? Where wrong view and greed arise together — these things are both bonds and united with bonds. ^1-1497

1498. What things are united with bonds but are not bonds? Eight mind-arisings accompanied by greed, two mind-arisings accompanied by dejection, except bonds that arise therein — these things are united with bonds but are not bonds. Things separated from bonds should not be said to be both bonds and united with bonds, nor united with bonds but not bonds. ^1-1498

1499. What things are separated from bonds and bondable? Greed that has arisen in four mind-arisings accompanied by greed, separated from wrong view, repulsion that has arisen in two mind-arisings accompanied by dejection, mind-arising accompanied by doubt, mind-arising accompanied by restlessness, wholesome in three levels, result in three levels, functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, and all form — these things are separated from bonds and bondable. ^1-1499

1500. What things are separated from bonds and not bondable? The four paths that are not included, the four noble-fruition results, and nibbana — these things are separated from bonds and not bondable. Things united with bonds should not be said to be separated from bonds and bondable, nor separated from bonds and not bondable. ^1-1500

##### Floods Group (summary) ^1-4-2-6-0

1501. What things are floods? The four floods … these things are separated from floods and floodable. ^1-1501

##### Yokes Group ^1-4-2-7-0

1502. What things are yokes? The four yokes … these things are separated from yokes and yokeable. ^1-1502

##### Hindrances Group ^1-4-2-8-0

1503. What things are hindrances? The six hindrances — hindrance of sense-desire, hindrance of ill will, hindrance of sloth and torpor, hindrance of restlessness and remorse, hindrance of doubt, hindrance of ignorance. The hindrance of sense-desire arises in eight mind-arisings accompanied by greed. The hindrance of ill will arises in two mind-arisings accompanied by dejection. The hindrance of sloth and torpor arises in unwholesome with applying effort. The hindrance of restlessness arises in mind-arisings accompanied by restlessness. The hindrance of remorse arises in two mind-arisings accompanied by dejection. The hindrance of doubt arises in mind-arisings accompanied by doubt. The hindrance of ignorance arises in all unwholesome — these things are hindrances. ^1-1503

1504. What things are not hindrances? Except hindrances, the remaining unwholesome, wholesome in four levels, result in four levels, functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, form, and nibbana — these things are not hindrances. ^1-1504

1505. What things are hinderable? Wholesome and unwholesome in three levels, result in three levels, functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, and all form — these things are hinderable. ^1-1505

1506. What things are not hinderable? The four paths that are not included, the four noble-fruition results, and nibbana — these things are not hinderable. ^1-1506

1507. What things are united with hindrances? Twelve unwholesome mind-arisings — these things are united with hindrances. ^1-1507

1508. What things are separated from hindrances? Wholesome in four levels, result in four levels, functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, form, and nibbana — these things are separated from hindrances. ^1-1508

1509. What things are both hindrances and hinderable? Those very hindrances are both hindrances and hinderable. ^1-1509

1510. What things are hinderable but are not hindrances? Except hindrances, the remaining unwholesome, wholesome in three levels, result in three levels, functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, and all form — these things are hinderable but are not hindrances. Things not hinderable should not be said to be both hindrances and hinderable, nor hinderable but not hindrances. ^1-1510

1511. What things are both hindrances and united with hindrances? Where two or three hindrances arise together — these things are both hindrances and united with hindrances. ^1-1511

1512. What things are united with hindrances but are not hindrances? Except hindrances, the remaining unwholesome — these things are united with hindrances but are not hindrances. Things separated from hindrances should not be said to be both hindrances and united with hindrances, nor united with hindrances but not hindrances. ^1-1512

1513. What things are separated from hindrances and hinderable? Wholesome in three levels, result in three levels, functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, and all form — these things are separated from hindrances and hinderable. ^1-1513

1514. What things are separated from hindrances and not hinderable? The four paths that are not included, the four noble-fruition results, and nibbana — these things are separated from hindrances and not hinderable. Things united with hindrances should not be said to be separated from hindrances and hinderable, nor separated from hindrances and not hinderable. ^1-1514

##### Clinging Group (views) ^1-4-2-9-0

1515. What things are clingings? Clinging through views arises in four mind-arisings united with wrong view — these things are clingings. ^1-1515

1516. What things are not clingings? Except clinging, the remaining unwholesome, wholesome in four levels, result in four levels, functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, form, and nibbana — these things are not clingings. ^1-1516

1517. What things are clingable? Wholesome and unwholesome in three levels, result in three levels, functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, and all form — these things are clingable. ^1-1517

1518. What things are not clingable? The four paths that are not included, the four noble-fruition results, and nibbana — these things are not clingable. ^1-1518

1519. What things are united with clingings? Four mind-arisings united with wrong view, except clinging that arises therein — these things are united with clingings. ^1-1519

1520. What things are separated from clingings? Four mind-arisings accompanied by greed, separated from wrong view, two mind-arisings accompanied by dejection, mind-arising accompanied by doubt, mind-arising accompanied by restlessness, wholesome in four levels, result in four levels, functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, form, and nibbana — these things are separated from clingings. Clinging should not be said to be united with clingings or separated from clingings. ^1-1520

1521. What things are both clingings and clingable? That very clinging is both clinging and clingable. ^1-1521

1522. What things are clingable but are not clingings? Except clinging, the remaining unwholesome, wholesome in three levels, result in three levels, functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, and all form — these things are clingable but are not clingings. Things not clingable should not be said to be both clingings and clingable, nor clingable but not clingings. ^1-1522

1523. What things are separated from clingings and clingable? Four mind-arisings accompanied by greed, separated from wrong view, two mind-arisings accompanied by dejection, mind-arising accompanied by doubt, mind-arising accompanied by restlessness, wholesome in three levels, result in three levels, functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, and all form — these things are separated from clingings and clingable. ^1-1523

1524. What things are separated from clingings and not clingable? The four paths that are not included, the four noble-fruition results, and nibbana — these things are separated from clingings and not clingable. Clingings and things united with clingings should not be said to be separated from clingings and clingable, nor separated from clingings and not clingable. ^1-1524

##### Large Interior Duals ^1-4-2-10-0

1525. What things have an object? Wholesome and unwholesome in four levels, result in four levels, functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels — these things have an object. ^1-1525

1526. What things have no object? Form and nibbana — these things have no object. ^1-1526

1527. What things are mind? Eye-consciousness, ear-consciousness, nose-consciousness, tongue-consciousness, body-consciousness, mind element, mind-consciousness element — these things are mind. ^1-1527

1528. What things are not mind? Aggregate of feeling, aggregate of perception, aggregate of habits and intentions, form, and nibbana — these things are not mind. ^1-1528

1529. What things are mental factors? Aggregate of feeling, aggregate of perception, aggregate of habits and intentions — these things are mental factors. ^1-1529

1530. What things are not mental factors? Mind, form, and nibbana — these things are not mental factors. ^1-1530

1531. What things are united with mind? Aggregate of feeling, aggregate of perception, aggregate of habits and intentions — these things are united with mind. ^1-1531

1532. What things are separated from mind? Form and nibbana — these things are separated from mind. Mind should not be said to be united with mind or separated from mind. ^1-1532

1533. What things are mixed with mind? Aggregate of feeling, aggregate of perception, aggregate of habits and intentions — these things are mixed with mind. ^1-1533

1534. What things are not mixed with mind? Form and nibbana — these things are not mixed with mind. Mind should not be said to be mixed with mind or not mixed with mind. ^1-1534

1535. What things arise from mind? Aggregate of feeling, aggregate of perception, aggregate of habits and intentions, bodily communication, verbal communication, and whatever other form there may be born of mind, with mind as cause, arising from mind — visible-form sense base, sound sense base, smell sense base, taste sense base, touch sense base, space element, water element, lightness of form, softness of form, readiness of form, growth of form, continuity of form, physical food — these things arise from mind. ^1-1535

1536. What things do not arise from mind? Mind, the remaining form, and nibbana — these things do not arise from mind. ^1-1536

1537. What things accompany mind? Aggregate of feeling, aggregate of perception, aggregate of habits and intentions, bodily communication, verbal communication — these things accompany mind. ^1-1537

1538. What things do not accompany mind? Mind, the remaining form, and nibbana — these things do not accompany mind. ^1-1538

1539. What things follow mind? Aggregate of feeling, aggregate of perception, aggregate of habits and intentions, bodily communication, verbal communication — these things follow mind. ^1-1539

1540. What things do not follow mind? Mind, the remaining form, and nibbana — these things do not follow mind. ^1-1540

1541. What things are mixed with mind and arise from mind? Aggregate of feeling, aggregate of perception, aggregate of habits and intentions — these things are mixed with mind and arise from mind. ^1-1541

1542. What things are not mixed with mind and do not arise from mind? Mind, form, and nibbana — these things are not mixed with mind and do not arise from mind. ^1-1542

1543. What things are mixed with mind, arise from mind, and accompany mind? Aggregate of feeling, aggregate of perception, aggregate of habits and intentions — these things are mixed with mind, arise from mind, and accompany mind. ^1-1543

1544. What things are not mixed with mind, do not arise from mind, and do not accompany mind? Mind, form, and nibbana — these things are not mixed with mind, do not arise from mind, and do not accompany mind. ^1-1544

1545. What things are mixed with mind, arise from mind, and follow mind? Aggregate of feeling, aggregate of perception, aggregate of habits and intentions — these things are mixed with mind, arise from mind, and follow mind. ^1-1545

1546. What things are not mixed with mind, do not arise from mind, and do not follow mind? Mind, form, and nibbana — these things are not mixed with mind, do not arise from mind, and do not follow mind. ^1-1546

1547. What things are internal? Eye sense base … mind sense base — these things are internal. ^1-1547

1548. What things are external? Visible-form sense base … mental-object sense base — these things are external. ^1-1548

1549. What things are derived? Eye sense base … physical food — these things are derived. ^1-1549

1550. What things are not derived? Wholesome and unwholesome in four levels, result in four levels, functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, the four great elements, and nibbana — these things are not derived. ^1-1550

1551. What things are taken up? Result in three levels, and whatever form is made by action — these things are taken up. ^1-1551

1552. What things are not taken up? Wholesome and unwholesome in three levels, functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, whatever form is not made by action, the four paths that are not included, the four noble-fruition results, and nibbana — these things are not taken up. ^1-1552

##### Clinging Group ^1-4-2-11-0

1553. What things are clingings? The four clingings — clinging to sense pleasures, clinging to views, clinging to virtue and practice, clinging to personality belief. Clinging to sense pleasures arises in eight mind-arisings accompanied by greed. Clinging to views, clinging to virtue and practice, and clinging to personality belief arise in four mind-arisings united with wrong view — these things are clingings. ^1-1553

1554. What things are not clingings? Except clingings, the remaining unwholesome, wholesome in four levels, result in four levels, functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, form, and nibbana — these things are not clingings. ^1-1554

1555. What things are clingable? Wholesome and unwholesome in three levels, result in three levels, functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, and all form — these things are clingable. ^1-1555

1556. What things are not clingable? The four paths that are not included, the four noble-fruition results, and nibbana — these things are not clingable. ^1-1556

1557. What things are united with clingings? Four mind-arisings united with wrong view accompanied by greed, four mind-arisings accompanied by greed, separated from wrong view, except greed that arises therein — these things are united with clingings. ^1-1557

1558. What things are separated from clingings? Greed that has arisen in four mind-arisings accompanied by greed, separated from wrong view, two mind-arisings accompanied by dejection, mind-arising accompanied by doubt, mind-arising accompanied by restlessness, wholesome in four levels, result in four levels, functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, form, and nibbana — these things are separated from clingings. ^1-1558

1559. What things are both clingings and clingable? Those very clingings are both clingings and clingable. ^1-1559

1560. What things are clingable but are not clingings? Except clingings, the remaining unwholesome, wholesome in three levels, result in three levels, functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, and all form — these things are clingable but are not clingings. Things not clingable should not be said to be both clingings and clingable, nor clingable but not clingings. ^1-1560

1561. What things are both clingings and united with clingings? Where wrong view and greed arise together — these things are both clingings and united with clingings. ^1-1561

1562. What things are united with clingings but are not clingings? Eight mind-arisings accompanied by greed, except clingings that arise therein — these things are united with clingings but are not clingings. Things separated from clingings should not be said to be both clingings and united with clingings, nor united with clingings but not clingings. ^1-1562

1563. What things are separated from clingings and clingable? Greed that has arisen in four mind-arisings accompanied by greed, separated from wrong view, two mind-arisings accompanied by dejection, mind-arising accompanied by doubt, mind-arising accompanied by restlessness, wholesome in three levels, result in three levels, functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, and all form — these things are separated from clingings and clingable. ^1-1563

1564. What things are separated from clingings and not clingable? The four paths that are not included, the four noble-fruition results, and nibbana — these things are separated from clingings and not clingable. Things united with clingings should not be said to be separated from clingings and clingable, nor separated from clingings and not clingable. ^1-1564

##### Defilements Group ^1-4-2-12-0

1565. What things are defilements? The ten bases of defilement — greed, hatred, delusion, pride, view, doubt, sloth, restlessness, without conscience, without worry about wrong action. Greed arises in eight mind-arisings accompanied by greed. Hatred arises in two mind-arisings accompanied by dejection. Delusion arises in all unwholesome. Pride arises in four mind-arisings accompanied by greed, separated from wrong view. View arises in four mind-arisings united with wrong view. Doubt arises in mind-arisings accompanied by doubt. Sloth arises in unwholesome with applying effort. Restlessness, without conscience, and without worry about wrong action arise in all unwholesome — these things are defilements. ^1-1565

1566. What things are not defilements? Except defilements, the remaining unwholesome, wholesome in four levels, result in four levels, functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, form, and nibbana — these things are not defilements. ^1-1566

1567. What things are defilementable? Wholesome and unwholesome in three levels, result in three levels, functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, and all form — these things are defilementable. ^1-1567

1568. What things are not defilementable? The four paths that are not included, the four noble-fruition results, and nibbana — these things are not defilementable. ^1-1568

1569. What things are stained? Twelve unwholesome mind-arisings — these things are stained. ^1-1569

1570. What things are unstained? Wholesome in four levels, result in four levels, functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, form, and nibbana — these things are unstained. ^1-1570

1571. What things are united with defilements? Twelve unwholesome mind-arisings — these things are united with defilements. ^1-1571

1572. What things are separated from defilements? Wholesome in four levels, result in four levels, functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, form, and nibbana — these things are separated from defilements. ^1-1572

1573. What things are both defilements and defilementable? Those very defilements are both defilements and defilementable. ^1-1573

1574. What things are defilementable but are not defilements? Except defilements, the remaining unwholesome, wholesome in three levels, result in three levels, functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, and all form — these things are defilementable but are not defilements. Things not defilementable should not be said to be both defilements and defilementable, nor defilementable but not defilements. ^1-1574

1575. What things are both defilements and stained? Those very defilements are both defilements and stained. ^1-1575

1576. What things are stained but are not defilements? Except defilements, the remaining unwholesome — these things are stained but are not defilements. Things unstained should not be said to be both defilements and stained, nor stained but not defilements. ^1-1576

1577. What things are both defilements and united with defilements? Where two or three defilements arise together — these things are both defilements and united with defilements. ^1-1577

1578. What things are united with defilements but are not defilements? Except defilements, the remaining unwholesome — these things are united with defilements but are not defilements. Things separated from defilements should not be said to be both defilements and united with defilements, nor united with defilements but not defilements. ^1-1578

1579. What things are separated from defilements and defilementable? Wholesome in three levels, result in three levels, functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, and all form — these things are separated from defilements and defilementable. ^1-1579

1580. What things are separated from defilements and not defilementable? The four paths that are not included, the four noble-fruition results, and nibbana — these things are separated from defilements and not defilementable. Things united with defilements should not be said to be separated from defilements and defilementable, nor separated from defilements and not defilementable. ^1-1580

##### Rear Dual Summary ^1-4-2-13-0

1581. What things are to be abandoned by seeing? Four mind-arisings united with wrong view, mind-arising accompanied by doubt — these things are to be abandoned by seeing. Four mind-arisings accompanied by greed, separated from wrong view, two mind-arisings accompanied by dejection — these things may be to be abandoned by seeing, may not be to be abandoned by seeing. ^1-1581

1582. What things are not to be abandoned by seeing? Mind-arising accompanied by restlessness, wholesome in four levels, result in four levels, functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, form, and nibbana — these things are not to be abandoned by seeing. ^1-1582

1583. What things are to be abandoned by development? Mind-arising accompanied by restlessness — these things are to be abandoned by development. Four mind-arisings accompanied by greed, separated from wrong view, two mind-arisings accompanied by dejection — these things may be to be abandoned by development, may not be to be abandoned by development. ^1-1583

1584. What things are not to be abandoned by development? Four mind-arisings united with wrong view, mind-arising accompanied by doubt, wholesome in four levels, result in four levels, functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, form, and nibbana — these things are not to be abandoned by development. ^1-1584

1585. What things are causes of things to be abandoned by seeing? Four mind-arisings united with wrong view, mind-arising accompanied by doubt, except delusion that arises therein — these things are causes of things to be abandoned by seeing. Four mind-arisings accompanied by greed, separated from wrong view, two mind-arisings accompanied by dejection — these things may be causes of things to be abandoned by seeing, may not be causes of things to be abandoned by seeing. ^1-1585

1586. What things are not causes of things to be abandoned by seeing? Delusion accompanied by doubt, mind-arising accompanied by restlessness, wholesome in four levels, result in four levels, functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, form, and nibbana — these things are not causes of things to be abandoned by seeing. ^1-1586

1587. What things are causes of things to be abandoned by development? Mind-arising accompanied by restlessness, except delusion that arises therein — these things are causes of things to be abandoned by development. Four mind-arisings accompanied by greed, separated from wrong view, two mind-arisings accompanied by dejection — these things may be causes of things to be abandoned by development, may not be causes of things to be abandoned by development. ^1-1587

1588. What things are not causes of things to be abandoned by development? Four mind-arisings united with wrong view, mind-arising accompanied by doubt, delusion accompanied by restlessness, wholesome in four levels, result in four levels, functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, form, and nibbana — these things are not causes of things to be abandoned by development. ^1-1588

1589. What things have thinking? Sense-desire-level wholesome, unwholesome, eleven mind-arisings from result of sense-desire-level wholesome, two from result of unwholesome, eleven functionally, form-level first absorption from wholesome, result, and functional, supramundane first absorption from wholesome and result, except thinking that arises therein — these things have thinking. ^1-1589

1590. What things have no thinking? The two sets of five consciousnesses, form-level second-through-fourth absorptions from wholesome, result, and functional, four formless levels from wholesome, result, and functional, supramundane second-through-fourth absorptions from wholesome and result, thinking, form, and nibbana — these things have no thinking. ^1-1590

1591. What things have examining? Sense-desire-level wholesome, unwholesome, eleven mind-arisings from result of sense-desire-level wholesome, two from result of unwholesome, eleven functionally, form-level first-and-second absorptions from wholesome, result, and functional, supramundane first-and-second absorptions from wholesome and result, except examining that arises therein — these things have examining. ^1-1591

1592. What things have no examining? The two sets of five consciousnesses, form-level second-through-fourth absorptions from wholesome, result, and functional, four formless levels from wholesome, result, and functional, supramundane second-through-fourth absorptions from wholesome and result, examining, form, and nibbana — these things have no examining. ^1-1592

1593. What things have a feeling of joy? From sense-desire-level wholesome, four mind-arisings accompanied by happiness; from unwholesome, four; from result of sense-desire-level wholesome, five; functionally, five; form-level first-and-second absorptions from wholesome, result, and functional; supramundane first-and-second absorptions from wholesome and result, except the feeling of joy that arises therein — these things have a feeling of joy. ^1-1593

1594. What things have no feeling of joy? From sense-desire-level wholesome, four mind-arisings accompanied by calm balance; from unwholesome, eight; from result of sense-desire-level wholesome, eleven; from result of unwholesome, seven; functionally, six; form-level fourth-and-fifth absorptions from wholesome, result, and functional, four formless levels from wholesome, result, and functional, supramundane fourth-and-fifth absorptions from wholesome and result, the feeling of joy, form, and nibbana — these things have no feeling of joy. ^1-1594

1595. What things are accompanied by a feeling of joy? From sense-desire-level wholesome, four mind-arisings accompanied by happiness; from unwholesome, four; from result of sense-desire-level wholesome, five; functionally, five; form-level first-and-second absorptions from wholesome, result, and functional; supramundane first-and-second absorptions from wholesome and result, except the feeling of joy that arises therein — these things are accompanied by a feeling of joy. ^1-1595

1596. What things are not accompanied by a feeling of joy? From sense-desire-level wholesome, four mind-arisings accompanied by calm balance; from unwholesome, eight; from result of sense-desire-level wholesome, eleven; from result of unwholesome, seven; functionally, six; form-level fourth-and-fifth absorptions from wholesome, result, and functional, four formless levels from wholesome, result, and functional, supramundane fourth-and-fifth absorptions from wholesome and result, the feeling of joy, form, and nibbana — these things are not accompanied by a feeling of joy. ^1-1596

1597. What things are accompanied by happiness? From sense-desire-level wholesome, four mind-arisings accompanied by happiness; from unwholesome, four; from result of sense-desire-level wholesome, six; functionally, five; form-level second-through-fourth absorptions from wholesome, result, and functional; supramundane second-through-fourth absorptions from wholesome and result, except happiness that arises therein — these things are accompanied by happiness. ^1-1597

1598. What things are not accompanied by happiness? From sense-desire-level wholesome, four mind-arisings accompanied by calm balance; from unwholesome, eight; from result of sense-desire-level wholesome, ten; from result of unwholesome, seven; functionally, six; form-level fourth absorption from wholesome, result, and functional, four formless levels from wholesome, result, and functional, supramundane fourth absorption from wholesome and result, happiness, form, and nibbana — these things are not accompanied by happiness. ^1-1598

1599. What things are accompanied by calm balance? From sense-desire-level wholesome, four mind-arisings accompanied by calm balance; from unwholesome, six; from result of sense-desire-level wholesome, ten; from result of unwholesome, six; functionally, six; form-level fourth absorption from wholesome, result, and functional, four formless levels from wholesome, result, and functional, supramundane fourth absorption from wholesome and result, except calm balance that arises therein — these things are accompanied by calm balance. ^1-1599

1600. What things are not accompanied by calm balance? From sense-desire-level wholesome, four mind-arisings accompanied by happiness; from unwholesome, six; from result of sense-desire-level wholesome, six; from result of unwholesome, one; functionally, five; form-level second-through-fourth absorptions from wholesome, result, and functional, supramundane second-through-fourth absorptions from wholesome and result, calm balance, form, and nibbana — these things are not accompanied by calm balance. ^1-1600

1601. What things belong to the sense-desire level? Sense-desire-level wholesome, unwholesome, all result of sense-desire level, sense-desire-level functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind, and all form — these things belong to the sense-desire level. ^1-1601

1602. What things do not belong to the sense-desire level? Form-level, formless-level, and what is not included — these things do not belong to the sense-desire level. ^1-1602

1603. What things belong to the form level? Form-level fourth-and-fifth absorptions from wholesome, result, and functional — these things belong to the form level. ^1-1603

1604. What things do not belong to the form level? Sense-desire-level, formless-level, and what is not included — these things do not belong to the form level. ^1-1604

1605. What things belong to the formless level? Four formless levels from wholesome, result, and functional — these things belong to the formless level. ^1-1605

1606. What things do not belong to the formless level? Sense-desire-level, form-level, and what is not included — these things do not belong to the formless level. ^1-1606

1607. What things are included? Wholesome and unwholesome in three levels, result in three levels, functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, and all form — these things are included. ^1-1607

1608. What things are not included? The four paths that are not included, the four noble-fruition results, and nibbana — these things are not included. ^1-1608

1609. What things lead out? The four paths that are not included — these things lead out. ^1-1609

1610. What things do not lead out? Wholesome and unwholesome in three levels, result in four levels, functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, form, and nibbana — these things do not lead out. ^1-1610

1611. What things are fixed? Four mind-arisings united with wrong view, two mind-arisings accompanied by dejection — these things may be fixed, may be not fixed. The four paths that are not included — these things are fixed. ^1-1611

1612. What things are not fixed? Four mind-arisings accompanied by greed, separated from wrong view, mind-arising accompanied by doubt, mind-arising accompanied by restlessness, wholesome in three levels, result in four levels, functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, form, and nibbana — these things are not fixed. ^1-1612

1613. What things have a lower? Wholesome and unwholesome in three levels, result in three levels, functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, and all form — these things have a lower. ^1-1613

1614. What things have no lower? The four paths that are not included, the four noble-fruition results, and nibbana — these things have no lower. ^1-1614

1615. What things are a refuge for bad states? Twelve unwholesome mind-arisings — these things are a refuge for bad states. ^1-1615

1616. What things are not a refuge for bad states? Wholesome in four levels, result in four levels, functional neither-good-nor-bad-for-the-mind in three levels, form, and nibbana — these things are not a refuge for bad states.
The Meaning Summary is finished.
The Dhammasaṅgaṇī treatise is finished. ^1-1616
